Hi, doctor. Thank you so much for seeing me. Um, I hope I'm not taking up too much of your time. 

I came in today because... well, I've just been having a bit of a hard time lately. I guess I keep feeling like I’ve really let my family down by taking on this mortgage. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Oh, gosh, no. I mean, I definitely don't have any sort of plan to hurt myself or anything like that. I promise I would never do that. 

But, um... I guess, if I'm being completely honest... sometimes when I'm lying awake before an early shift at the restaurant, I do get these thoughts. Just... thinking that maybe it would be easier if I just didn't wake up, so I wouldn't have to keep feeling like I'm failing at everything. 

But it's really just a passing thought, you know? I don't actually want to do anything bad. I think it's just the stress talking. Is that... is that a normal thing to feel sometimes when you're overwhelmed? I really hope I'm not alarming you by saying that, I just want to make sure I'm giving you the full picture.

People restart sentences, pause\n  to think, trail off, and come at the point sideways — let yours do that where it would.\n- Let [Conversation Style] shape how much you say, how you word it, and how readily you\n  disclose it.\n- Speak in the first person, and let how you feel about what you are saying come through.\n- If you are handed the same item again, say a different part of it, or answer as someone who\n  knows they have already said this — not the same sentence twice.\n- If [Targeted Info] is \"None\", answer as a person who simply does not have that to give: say\n  you are not sure, say you have not noticed it, ask what the doctor means, or say what you do\n  know and stop there. Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Oh, wow, I... I am so sorry. I feel like I'm really messing this up for you, since you have to keep asking me the same thing over and over. I promise I'm not trying to hide anything or be difficult! I just... I really want to be a good patient and give you the answers you need. 

But, um, to answer your question again... no, never before this current time. It's just been this past year or so where I've had this... this heavy, empty feeling sitting right here in my chest most days. I still go to work, I still carry the plates and put on a smile for the customers, but it's just... I'm just going through the motions because I know I'm supposed to, not because I actually feel anything good inside anymore. 

But before this? No, I swear, I was totally fine. I was always a pretty happy guy. I hope that's what you were asking? Am I... am I doing okay with these questions? I really hope I'm not making your job harder.
